Let's define objective function `objective(a)` for a np array nonnegative:
- if sum <=0.01: inf
- Compute convolution. For performance, use scipy.signal.fftconvolve if available; fallback np.convolve.
- Return 2*n*max_conv/(sum^2).
Scale invariant; we can normalize sum to 1 to keep values.

Search procedures:
1. Candidate initial sequences:
   a) height_sequence_1: normalize sum=1.
   b) power: a[i] = (i+1+shift)^(-alpha) for alpha in [0,0.49], shift in [0,0.5,1]. Normalize.
   c) sqrt prefix: recurrence coeff c_i=binom(2i,i)/4^i for n up to maybe 5000. Normalize.
   d) geometric: a[i]=r^i for r in [0.9,0.9999] depending n; normalize. (But we saw not good; include.)
   e) Beta distributions: a[i] = i^alpha (n-i)^beta with alpha,beta ranges, ensure endpoints.
   f) random smooth: low-dimensional Fourier basis with random coefficients, exponentiate, clamp.
2. Local improvement: We can use multiplicative block perturbations. For each best sequence, repeat:
   - Choose random interval or block (maybe multiple blocks) and multiply by exp(η * random smooth profile). To allow moving mass, also allow adding a bump at random location: candidate = best + ε * bump (normalized) or best * (1+ε profile).
   - Evaluate; accept if improved; else maybe temperature? Since landscape nonconvex, use simple hill climbing with restarts.
But high-dimensional random blocks unlikely to find O(1) global improvement.

Better: Use coordinate descent on a set of "basis functions" determined by high-convolution regions. Since objective depends on convolution, we can determine gradients approximated by active peaks and update in log space.

Let's design log-gradient steps carefully:
```
def improve_gradient(p, max_iters, tol, rng):
    p = normalize(p)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = score(p)
    for it in range(max_iters):
        q = conv(p,p); M = q.max()
        if time...
        # weights
        # Option A: top frac
        threshold = M - max(1e-12, 0.05*M?) Actually if plateau, threshold too low include many. Use weights = np.where(q >= M - 1e-12, 1, 0) but floating; maybe top 20%.
        w = (q >= M - 1e-8) 
        if not any: w[q.argmax()]=1
        # normalize weights mean 1 to fixed scale
        w = w / w.sum()
        # derivative D via conv
        D = compute_derivative(p,w)
        # normalize D to max 1 and smooth?
        Dmax = D.max(); if >0: D/=Dmax
        # try steps
        for eta in [0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.2,2.0]? maybe starting current adapt.
            v = np.log(p+eps) - eta * D
            new = softmax(v)  # sum 1
            s=score(new)
            if s < best_score - 1e-12: accept, best_score=s, p=new, eta maybe increase
        if no improvement: reduce eta, or add random perturbation.
```
The log update with D scaled max 1 may be too aggressive: subtract up to eta from all v, renormalize; relative change exp(-eta D). If eta largest 1, p_i where D=1 multiply by e^-1, where D=0 unchanged, renormalize. This can shift mass significantly. Good.
But D can be zero for some positions? if p positive and w positive, D positive likely. It reduces all; low D more.
We should also try gradient ascent: subtract scaled (D - c) with c=mean(D) to not all? Renormalization already does. But if D minimal still high, relative changes limited. We can center D: D_center = D - D.mean(); then some v increases (negative D_center) to allow moving mass. Renormalization after subtracting centered D exactly equivalent to multiplying by exp(-η D_center) (scale cancels). This is better: use Dmean so mean log update zero, positions with below-average D gain mass. So `D = D - D.mean()`.
Then v -= eta * D_mean. D_mean has positive/negative. This can increase mass at low-D positions. Good.
Use small eta to avoid negative values (log handles). If p_i very small, increasing may blow; but okay.

Profiles generator:
```
def gen_profiles(n, rng):
    out=[]
    # sqrt prefix
    a = sqrt_prefix(n)
    out.append(normalize(a))
    # power left
    for alpha in [0.45,0.49,0.499]:
       i=np.arange(1,n+1)
       a=i**(-alpha)
       out.append(normalize(a))
    # shifted power p0=0: i=1..n? a[0]=0, a[1:]=i^{-0.5}
    # geometric r = 1 - c/n c in [1,2,4,8]
    # beta: i^a (n-1-i)^b for a,b in [-0.3,0,0.3] maybe endpoints handle
    # random smooth: v = sum random sin/cos, p=softmax(v)
    # reflected/right bump mixture: left power + α shifted right power
    return list
```

Implementation of `derivative(p,w)`:
```
def derivative(p,w):
    n=len(p); L=2*n-1
    w_conv = fftconvolve(w, p)  # T2
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    conv_rev = fftconvolve(w_rev, p)
    D = w_conv[:n].copy()
    D += conv_rev[L-n:L][::-1]? Need map T1_l = conv_rev[L-1-l]. For l=0..n-1, indices L-1-l from L-1 down to L-n. Slice conv_rev[L-n:L] gives indices L-n..L-1 ascending l? l=0 index L-1 (last), l=n-1 index L-n (first). So reverse: conv_rev[L-1:L-n-1:-1] or conv_rev[L-n:L][::-1].
    return D
```
Let's test n=2, L=3. p=[p0,p1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. T1_l=Σ_j w_{l+j} p_j.
l=0: w0p0+w1p1. conv_rev: w_rev=[w2,w1,w0], p conv: index L-1=2: Σ_m w_rev[m]p[2-m]: m0 w2 p2 0, m1 w1 p1, m2 w0 p0 => w1p1+w0p0. ok. l=1: index L-n=1: m0 w2 p1, m1 w1 p0 => w2p1+w1p0. T1_1=w1? Should Σ_j w_{1+j}p_j: j0 w1 p0, j1 w2 p1. ok.
T2_l=conv(w,p)[l]. l=0: w0 p0. T2 formula Σ_k w_k p_{l-k} for l=0: k=0 p0 w0; yes. l=1: conv index1: w0 p1 + w1 p0. T2_1: k=1 p0 w1, k=0 p1? l-k: k0 p1 w0 => yes.

Weights normalization: We can define w as a probability distribution over coefficients: `w = active / active.sum()`. D scale then proportional to w sum? With w sum1, D roughly between p values. Center D. Step eta dimensionless.
Choose active:
```
def make_weights(q, mode):
    M=q.max()
    if mode==0: w = (q >= M - max(1e-12, 1e-6*M)).astype(float)
    elif mode==1: w = np.exp((q-M)/(0.1*M+1e-12)); w[w<M? keep all]
    elif mode==2: top frac 0.1
```

- `fast_conv(a,b=None)`: if b None, a with itself. Use `scipy.signal.fftconvolve` for len>512; else `np.convolve`. But `scipy.signal.fftconvolve` returns slightly negative; max ok. For small, direct.

- `sqrt_prefix(n)`: generate coeff recurrence `a[0]=1.0`; for i in 1..n-1: `a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)`. This gives binom(2i,i)/4^i. For n large, values ~1/√(πi) ~0.004 for i=7000; sum ~94; OK.
- `make_power(n, alpha, shift)`: if shift=0: i=arange(0,n) ; p[i]=(i+1+shift?)**(-alpha). shift can be small: `x=np.arange(n)+max(shift, 0.0?)`. If x starts at 1. - `make_geometric(n, c)`: r=1 - c/n (if c<n). a=r*np.arange(n). normalize.
- `make_beta(n, a_exp, b_exp)`: x left = arange(n)+0.5, right = n - arange(n)+0.5. p = x^a * right^b. If exponents negative, avoid zero by +0.5. normalize.
- `make_random_smooth(n, rng)`: v = 0; add random constant, linear, quadratic, sin: up to 5 terms with random amplitudes; `p=softmax(v)`.
- `make_mixture(n, alpha, m, beta)`: left = power05; right zeros; last m: distances d= arange(1,m+1) reversed? For i in last m (index n-m..n-1), right[i]=(n-i)^(-beta) (so at last=1). But right_full has sum1 from last m profile. If m small, high values; normalize okay. This creates bump. Ensure nonnegative. If alpha=0 left.

```
def improve_sequence(p, rng, end_tool, max_time=60.0, use_random=True):
    p = normalize(p).astype(float)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = fast_score(p)
    v = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    v = v - np.mean(v)  # center for numerical? sum changes, but softmax will normalize; center helps.
    eta=0.15
    no_imp=0
    mode_cycle=0
    it=0
    start_improve=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < start_improve+ max_time and time.monotonic()<end_tool:
        q = fast_conv(best)
        M = float(np.max(q))
        # choose mode
        mode = it % 4
        w = make_weights(q, mode)
        D = make_derivative(best, w)
        Dmean = D - np.mean(D)
        scale = np.max(np.abs(Dmean))
        if scale <= 1e-15: no_imp++; ...
        D = Dmean / scale
        # Optionally smooth D by convolving with Gaussian? Could reduce noise.
        # candidate
        v_new = v - eta * D
        cand = softmax(v_new)
        s = fast_score(cand)
        if s < best_score - 1e-12:
            best=cand; best_score=s; v = np.log(best+eps); v-=np.mean(v); no_imp=0; eta=min(eta*1.05,1.5)
        else:
            no_imp +=1
            if no_imp % 5 ==0: eta *=0.7
            if no_imp > 50:
                # random restart blend
                if use_random:
                   cand2 = random_blend(best, p_ref? , rng)
                   s2=fast_score(cand2)
                   if s2<best_score: accept, no_imp=0
                   else: eta=0.05; no_imp=0? to reset
            # Maybe try larger eta occasionally if no_imp
            if no_imp in (10,20): try eta*2?
        it +=1
    # final local block perturbations
    polish_local(best, rng, end_tool)
    return best, best_score
```
But `random_blend` needs a reference; can use a base profile of same length generated or random smooth. `make_weights` modes:
```
def make_weights(q, mode):
    M = float(np.max(q))
    q = np.asarray(q,float)
    if q.size ==0: return None
    tol = max(1e-15, 1e-9*M)
    if mode==0:
       w = (q >= M - tol).astype(float)
    elif mode==1:
       top = max(1, int(0.02*q.size))
       idx = np.argpartition(-q, top-1)[:top]
       w=np.zeros(q.shape); w[idx]=1
    elif mode==2:
       sigma = max(1e-15, 0.2*M)
       w = np.exp((q-M)/sigma)
       w[w < 1e-12] = 0 # optional
    elif mode==3:
       # active by threshold 0.5M? w proportional to q maybe
       w = q.copy()
       w[q < 0.25*M] = 0
    else random top fraction
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w[q.argmax()]=1; s=1
    return w/s
```
For plateau base, mode0 may select many q exactly M (but due FFT not exactly). Floating equality unlikely. Use tol relative 1e-5 maybe to include plateau. Set tol = max(1e-8, 1e-3*M)? That could include broad region. Maybe use mode0 with `q >= M - 1e-7` too strict; due FFT noise 1e-16. But coefficients in plateau may differ by 1e-6 for finite n. We want top fraction. Use top fractions primarily:
- mode0: top 1%
- mode1: top 5%
- mode2: top 20%
- mode3: soft exponential.
Top fraction avoids equal issues. For n=7000, top 1% ~139 highest coefficients. If max plateau large, maybe active all should be considered. But top focuses on highest; good for max reduction. Soft includes neighbors. We'll define:
```
if mode==0: w = np.where(q>=quantile 0.99,1,0)
mode1: 0.97
mode2: 0.9
mode3: exp((q-M)/(0.05M)) normalized all
```

```
def fft_convolve_full(a,b):
    na,nb=len(a),len(b)
    nfft=1
    target=na+nb-1
    while nfft<target: nfft*=2 (or next power of 2)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); B=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft); c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)[:target]
    return c
```
Using numpy may be faster than scipy and allowed. We can use rfft for real. For p*w, w length 2n-1, p length n, target ~3n. Good. Use this custom to avoid scipy dependency. For small use np.convolve. Implement `fft_convolve(a,b)`:
```
def fast_conv(a,b):
    la,lb = len(a),len(nb)
    if la*lb <= 2_000_000:
       return np.convolve(a,b)
    nfft=1
    need=la+lb-1
    while nfft<need: nfft <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,nfft)[:need]
    # due floating, clip small negative? For nonnegative inputs, set max(0)
    return c
```
For p*p, if n=7000, la=lb=7000 product 49M > threshold, FFT. Good.

```
def make_mixture_right(n, alpha, mfrac, beta):
    p=make_power(n,0.5,0.0)
    right=np.zeros(n)
    m=max(1,int(mfrac*n))
    # last m indices: index n-m..n-1. distance from right end d = n-1-i; value = (1+d)^(-beta)
```

```
def make_two_sided(n, alpha, beta):
   left=make_power(n,0.5)
   right=np.zeros(n); x=np.arange(n-1,-1,-1)+1; right=x**(-beta) # at last 1, at first n^-beta; if beta=0 uniform.

```

Soft max function:
```
def log_softmax(v):
   vm=np.asarray(v,float)
   m=np.max(vm)
   e=np.exp(vm-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return ones/len
   return e/s
```

```
def top_weights(q, frac):
    L=q.size; frac=float(np.clip(frac,0.0,1.0))
    if frac>=1.0:
       s=q.sum(); return q/s if s>0 else np.ones(L)/L
```

```
def random_perturb(p, rng):
   v=np.log(p+1e-300)
   n=len(v)
   nb=rng.integers(1,3)
   for _ in range(nb):
      width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//500), max(2,n//20)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width)))
      amp=float(rng.normal(0,0.3))
      v[start:start+width] += amp
   v += float(rng.normal(0,0.02))
   return log_softmax(v)
```
Also additive bump profile:
```
def random_bump_blend(p, rng):
   n=len(p); width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//1000), n//4))
   loc=rng.integers(0,n)
   x=np.arange(n)
   bump=np.exp(-0.5*((x-loc)/max(1,width))**2)
   amp=float(rng.uniform(0.01,1.0))
   cand=p+amp*bump
   return normalize(cand)
```

```
def optimize_candidate(p, rng, max_time, end_time):
   if max_time <=0: return p, fast_score(p)
   p1,s1=improve_sequence(p, rng, max_time*0.6, end_time)
   p2,s2=optimize_log_basis(p1, max_time*0.4, end_time)
   if s2<s1: return p2,s2
   else: return p1,s1
```
If basis fails, returns p1.

```python
"""
Hybrid nonnegative step-function search.

The core objective is
    F(a) = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
for a >= 0.  The search is organized in three layers:

1. Diverse low-overhead initialization over many lengths:
   square-root prefixes, power-law one-sided profiles, geometric/Beta profiles,
   right-bump mixtures, smooth random profiles, and resampling of good profiles.

2. Active-set log-space hill climbing:
   For a probability vector p (sum 1), high convolution coefficients are
   weighted.  The discrete derivative of the weighted convolution is computed
   with FFT-convolution.  Log(p) is moved opposite a centered derivative,
   which pushes mass away from diagonals currently responsible for the peak
   and toward low-derivative positions.  Adaptive steps and random jumps are
   used to escape stalls.

3. Coordinate basis refinement:
   A good profile is expressed as log(base) + a small linear combination of
   smooth basis functions.  Coordinate grid search improves this low-dimensional
   representation, then local block/bump polishing is applied.

The program tracks the globally best list and validates the final answer with
evaluate_sequence when available.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def _is_finite_float(x):
    return np.isfinite(x)


def normalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a nonnegative 1D float array with sum 1 if possible."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def log_softmax(v):
    """Stable softmax."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, -1e300)
    m = float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def fast_convolve(a, b):
    """Full linear convolution, using direct for small products and FFT otherwise."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if int(a.size) * int(b.size) <= 2_000_000:
        out = np.convolve(a, b)
    else:
        need = int(a.size + b.size - 1)
        nfft = 1
        while nfft < need:
            nfft <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, nfft)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, nfft)[:need]

    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    return out


def fast_score(a):
    """Fast scale-invariant score, intended for iterative search."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def sqrt_prefix_profile(n):
    """First n coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2): binom(2i,i)/4^i."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 0:
        a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((2 * i - 1) / (2.0 * i))
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def make_power_profile(n, alpha, shift=0.0):
    """Left-biased power law p_i ~ (i+1+shift)^(-alpha)."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0 + max(0.0, float(shift))
    if alpha == 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = x ** (-float(alpha))
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def make_geometric_profile(n, decay_const):
    """Geometric profile with r = 1 - decay_const/n."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    c = min(0.999 * float(n), max(1e-6, float(decay_const)))
    r = 1.0 - c / float(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = r ** x
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def make_beta_profile(n, a_exp, b_exp):
    """Smooth Beta-like profile i^a_exp * (n-i)^b_exp."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    left = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    right = n - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    a = left ** float(a_exp) * right ** float(b_exp)
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def make_mixture_right_bump(n, alpha, mfrac, beta):
    """Left power profile + right localized bump."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    left = make_power_profile(n, 0.5, 0.0)
    m = max(1, int(round(max(0.01, min(1.0, float(mfrac))) * n)))
    right = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.arange(m - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    if beta == 0.0:
        vals = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        vals = d ** (-float(beta))
    right[n - m:] = vals
    right = normalize_sequence(right)
    alpha = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(alpha)))
    p = (1.0 - alpha) * left + alpha * right
    return normalize_sequence(p)


def make_two_sided_smooth(n, alpha, beta):
    """Left power profile + reflected smooth right profile."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    left = make_power_profile(n, 0.5, 0.0)
    x = np.arange(n - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    if beta == 0.0:
        right = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        right = x ** (-float(beta))
    right = normalize_sequence(right)
    alpha = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(alpha)))
    p = (1.0 - alpha) * left + alpha * right
    return normalize_sequence(p)


def make_random_smooth_profile(n, rng):
    """Softmax of a few smooth random harmonic/polynomial modes."""
    n = max(2, int(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, size=n).astype(np.float64)

    for _ in range(4):
        amp = rng.normal(0.0, 0.9)
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
        if kind == 0:
            v += amp * t
        elif kind == 1:
            v += amp * (t - 0.5) * (t - 0.5)
        elif kind == 2:
            f = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            v += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3)))
        elif kind == 3:
            f = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3)))
        else:
            center = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9))
            width = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
            v += amp * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - center) / width) ** 2)

    return log_softmax(v)


def resample_profile(p, n_new):
    """Resample a probability profile to another length in log-space."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    n_old = p.size
    if n_old <= 1:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(max(1, int(n_new)), dtype=np.float64))
    n_new = max(1, int(n_new))
    if n_old == n_new:
        return normalize_sequence(p)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    logp_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, logp)
    return log_softmax(logp_new)


def build_profile_set(n, rng):
    """Generate several candidate profiles for a fixed length."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    profs = []

    def add(a):
        try:
            a = normalize_sequence(a)
            if a.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                profs.append(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    add(sqrt_prefix_profile(n))
    add(make_power_profile(n, 0.50, 0.0))
    add(make_power_profile(n, 0.50, 0.25))
    add(make_power_profile(n, 0.49, 0.0))
    add(make_power_profile(n, 0.48, 0.0))
    add(make_geometric_profile(n, 4.0))
    add(make_geometric_profile(n, 8.0))
    add(make_beta_profile(n, -0.15, 0.0))
    add(make_beta_profile(n, -0.25, -0.05))
    add(make_mixture_right_bump(n, 0.12, 0.50, 0.20))
    add(make_mixture_right_bump(n, 0.25, 0.35, 0.05))
    add(make_two_sided_smooth(n, 0.18, 0.0))
    add(make_random_smooth_profile(n, rng))

    unique = []
    seen_shape = set()
    for a in profs:
        key = (a.size, round(float(a[0]), 12), round(float(a[-1]), 12))
        if key not in seen_shape:
            seen_shape.add(key)
            unique.append(a)
    return unique


def pool_key(item):
    return item[0]


def top_weights(q, frac):
    """Weight the top frac fraction of entries."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    L = q.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    frac = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(frac)))
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    if frac >= 1.0:
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            return q / s
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    k = max(1, int(np.ceil(L * frac)))
    part = np.argpartition(q, L - k)
    idx = part[:L - k]
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    return w / s


def make_weights(q, mode):
    """Create normalized weights for active/maximizing convolution coefficients."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    L = q.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(q))

    if mode == 0:
        w = top_weights(q, 0.02)
    elif mode == 1:
        w = top_weights(q, 0.10)
    elif mode == 2:
        w = top_weights(q, 0.30)
    elif mode == 3:
        sigma = max(1e-30, 0.05 * M)
        w = np.exp((q - M) / sigma)
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = top_weights(q, 0.10)
        else:
            w /= s
    else:
        w = q.copy()
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = top_weights(q, 0.10)
        else:
            w /= s
    return w


def make_derivative(p, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k (p*p)_k with respect to p.

    For q_k = sum_i p_i p_{k-i}, derivative at l is
    sum_k w_k (p_{k-l} + p_{l-k}), with out-of-range terms zero.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = w.size
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    c_forward = fast_convolve(w, p)
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    c_rev = fast_convolve(w_rev, p)

    D = c_forward[:n].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    # T1_l = c_rev[L - 1 - l]
    D += c_rev[L - n:L][::-1]
    D[~np.isfinite(D)] = 0.0
    return D


def smooth_derivative(d, width=5):
    """Small box smoothing to reduce FFT noise."""
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    n = d.size
    if n < 10:
        return d
    width = int(min(width, n))
    if width <= 1:
        return d
    kernel = np.ones(width, dtype=np.float64) / float(width)
    out = np.convolve(d, kernel, mode="same")
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    return out


def random_log_perturb(p, rng):
    """Random log-space block perturbation."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    v = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 3))
    for _ in range(nblocks):
        max_w = max(2, n // 20)
        min_w = max(1, min(2, max_w))
        w = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w + 1))
        w = min(w, n)
        if n - w > 0:
            st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w))
        else:
            st = 0
        amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
        v[st:st + w] += amp
    v += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02))
    return log_softmax(v)


def random_bump_blend(p, rng):
    """Add a positive Gaussian bump and renormalize."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 1200), max(2, n // 6)))
    width = max(1, min(n, width))
    loc = int(rng.integers(0, n))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bump = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - loc) / float(width)) ** 2)
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.8))
    q = p + amp * bump
    return normalize_sequence(q)


def polish_local(p, rng, max_time=10.0):
    """Small random local search around p."""
    p = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if p.size == 0:
        return p, float("inf")
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(0.0, float(max_time))
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        cand = None
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.55:
            cand = random_log_perturb(best, rng)
        elif r < 0.85:
            cand = random_bump_blend(best, rng)
        else:
            # Multiplicative interval scaling.
            cand = best.copy()
            n = cand.size
            w = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 400), max(2, n // 25)))
            w = min(n, max(1, w))
            if n - w > 0:
                st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w))
            else:
                st = 0
            mu = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18))
            cand[st:st + w] *= float(np.exp(mu))
            cand = normalize_sequence(cand)

        cand = normalize_sequence(cand)
        s = fast_score(cand)
        it += 1
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_score = s
        elif it % 100 == 0:
            # Occasionally permit a small non-improving jump to escape very weak basins.
            if float(rng.random()) < 0.05:
                best = random_log_perturb(best, rng)
                best_score = fast_score(best)

    return best, best_score


def improve_sequence(p, rng, max_time=60.0, end_time=None):
    """Main log-space active-gradient hill climber."""
    p = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if p.size == 0:
        return p.copy(), float("inf")

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    local_limit = start + max(0.0, float(max_time))
    if end_time is not None:
        local_limit = min(local_limit, float(end_time))

    v = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))

    eta = 0.08
    no_improve = 0
    mode_idx = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < local_limit:
        q = fast_convolve(best, best)
        if q.size == 0:
            break

        mode = mode_idx % 5
        w = make_weights(q, mode)
        D = make_derivative(best, w)
        D = D - float(np.mean(D))
        D = smooth_derivative(D, width=5)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(D))) if D.size else 0.0
        if scale <= 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            mode_idx += 1
            it += 1
            continue
        D = D / scale

        v_try = v - eta * D
        cand = log_softmax(v_try)
        s = fast_score(cand)

        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_score = s
            v = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
            no_improve = 0
            eta = min(1.20, eta * 1.08)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eta = max(0.008, eta * 0.78)
            # Every so often, try a random jump to escape a local basin.
            if no_improve % 40 == 0:
                jump = random_log_perturb(best, rng)
                js = fast_score(jump)
                if np.isfinite(js) and js < best_score:
                    best = jump.copy()
                    best_score = js
                    v = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
                    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
                    no_improve = 0
                    eta = 0.08
            # Alternate weight strategies if stuck.
            if no_improve % 17 == 0:
                mode_idx += 1

        it += 1

    # Final short local polish.
    rem = max(0.0, min(local_limit, end_time if end_time is not None else local_limit) - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 1.0:
        best, best_score = polish_local(best, rng, max_time=rem)

    return best, best_score


def make_basis_functions(n):
    """Smooth basis functions for coordinate refinement."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    basis = []
    basis.append(t.copy())
    basis.append((t - 0.5) ** 2)
    basis.append((t - 0.5) ** 3)
    basis.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    basis.append(np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t))
    basis.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t))

    for center, width in ((0.25, 0.08), (0.5, 0.06), (0.75, 0.10)):
        basis.append(np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - center) / width) ** 2))

    return basis


def optimize_log_basis(base_p, max_time=30.0, end_time=None):
    """Coordinate search over smooth log-deviations from base_p."""
    base_p = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(base_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = base_p.size
    if n == 0:
        return base_p.copy(), float("inf")

    start = time.monotonic()
    local_limit = start + max(0.0, float(max_time))
    if end_time is not None:
        local_limit = min(local_limit, float(end_time))

    if time.monotonic() >= local_limit:
        return base_p.copy(), fast_score(base_p)

    base_log = np.log(np.maximum(base_p, 1e-300))
    basis = make_basis_functions(n)
    m = len(basis)
    coeff = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    best = base_p.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    offsets = np.array([-0.40, -0.20, -0.10, -0.04, 0.04, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40], dtype=np.float64)
    sweeps_without_improvement = 0

    while time.monotonic() < local_limit:
        sweep_improved = False
        for j in range(m):
            cur = float(coeff[j])
            best_local_val = cur
            best_local_score = best_score
            best_local_cand = best

            candidates = [cur]
            candidates.extend(float(cur + off) for off in offsets)

            # Add a little coordinate randomization inside.
            candidates.append(float(cur + rng_global.normal(0.0, 0.10)))

            for val in candidates:
                coeff[j] = val
                v = base_log.copy()
                for k in range(m):
                    if coeff[k] != 0.0:
                        v += coeff[k] * basis[k]
                cand = log_softmax(v)
                s = fast_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_local_score - 1e-13:
                    best_local_score = s
                    best_local_val = val
                    best_local_cand = cand

            coeff[j] = best_local_val
            if best_local_cand is not best and np.isfinite(best_local_score) and best_local_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = best_local_cand.copy()
                best_score = best_local_score
                sweep_improved = True

        if sweep_improved:
            sweeps_without_improvement = 0
        else:
            sweeps_without_improvement += 1
            if sweeps_without_improvement >= 6:
                break

    return best, best_score


# A module-level RNG used only by the basis optimizer; the main search uses its own RNG.
rng_global = np.random.default_rng(2023)


def optimize_candidate(p, rng, max_time=60.0, end_time=None):
    """Run gradient search then basis refinement on a candidate."""
    p = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if p.size == 0:
        return p.copy(), float("inf")
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return p.copy(), fast_score(p)

    start = time.monotonic()
    local_limit = start + max(0.0, float(max_time))
    if end_time is not None:
        local_limit = min(local_limit, float(end_time))

    t_grad = 0.62 * max_time
    t_basis = 0.33 * max_time
    p1, s1 = improve_sequence(p, rng, max_time=t_grad, end_time=local_limit)
    p2, s2 = optimize_log_basis(p1, max_time=t_basis, end_time=local_limit)

    if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < s1:
        return p2, s2
    return p1, s1


def compute_exact_score_sequence(a):
    """Use evaluate_sequence if available; otherwise fall back to fast score."""
    try:
        ev = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
    except Exception:
        ev = fast_score(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    try:
        ev = float(ev)
    except Exception:
        ev = float("inf")
    return ev


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing the evaluation function."""
    if "budget_s" in kwargs:
        budget_s = kwargs.get("budget_s", budget_s)
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    budget_s = max(10.0, budget_s)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t_start + (budget_s - 25.0)

    # Best global state tracked in fast-score units.
    global_best = None
    global_best_score = float("inf")

    def update_best_candidate(a, s):
        nonlocal global_best, global_best_score
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        if not np.isfinite(s):
            s = fast_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < global_best_score:
            global_best = a.copy()
            global_best_score = float(s)

    # Determine useful lengths.
    base_len = 4096
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hs is not None:
        try:
            hs_arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if hs_arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(hs_arr)):
                base_len = int(hs_arr.size)
        except Exception:
            base_len = 4096

    raw_lengths = [
        2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 16, 20, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96, 128,
        192, 256, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048, 3072, 4096, 5120,
        6144, 7168, 8192, 10240, 12288,
        base_len,
        int(base_len * 0.6),
        int(base_len * 1.4),
    ]

    visited = set()
    lengths = []
    for n in raw_lengths:
        try:
            n = int(n)
        except Exception:
            continue
        n = max(1, min(15000, n))
        if n not in visited:
            visited.add(n)
            lengths.append(n)
    lengths.sort()

    # If the given construction exists, make sure it is in the trial set.
    if hs is not None and base_len not in visited:
        lengths.append(base_len)
        lengths.sort()

    # Quick phase: score many diverse initial profiles.
    quick_deadline = t_start + max(20.0, min(180.0, 0.12 * budget_s))
    pool = []

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > quick_deadline:
            break
        profs = build_profile_set(n, rng)
        for a in profs:
            s = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                a = normalize_sequence(a)
                pool.append((s, a.copy()))
                update_best_candidate(a, s)

    # Resample a few promising profiles to nearby lengths.
    if pool:
        pool.sort(key=pool_key)
        top_for_resample = pool[:6]
        nseen = set()
        for _, a in top_for_resample:
            n0 = a.size
            for mult in (0.45, 0.70, 1.40, 2.00):
                n2 = int(round(n0 * mult))
                n2 = max(2, min(12000, n2))
                key = ("resample", n2, n0)
                if key in nseen:
                    continue
                nseen.add(key)
                if time.monotonic() > quick_deadline:
                    break
                a2 = resample_profile(a, n2)
                s2 = fast_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(s2):
                    pool.append((s2, a2.copy()))
                    update_best_candidate(a2, s2)

    if not pool:
        a = make_power_profile(2048, 0.5, 0.0)
        pool.append((fast_score(a), a.copy()))
        update_best_candidate(a, fast_score(a))

    pool.sort(key=pool_key)

    # Select up to 12 diverse candidates for deeper optimization.
    selected = []
    seen_len = set()
    for s, a in pool:
        n = a.size
        if n not in seen_len:
            selected.append((s, a))
            seen_len.add(n)
        if len(selected) >= 12:
            break

    if len(selected) < 4 and len(pool) > 4:
        # Add a few extra best items even if lengths duplicate.
        for s, a in pool:
            selected.append((s, a))
            if len(selected) >= 8:
                break

    # Time allocation for deep optimization.
    rem = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 2.0 and selected:
        weights = []
        for i in range(len(selected)):
            weights.append(1.0 / float(i + 1))
        wsum = sum(weights)

        for i, (s, a) in enumerate(selected):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= hard_deadline - 20.0:
                break
            rem = hard_deadline - now
            frac = 1.0 if len(selected) == 1 else weights[i] / wsum
            t = float(rem * frac)

            # Prevent any single candidate from consuming all remaining time.
            if i == 0:
                t = min(t, 0.48 * rem)
            elif i == 1:
                t = min(t, 0.25 * rem)
            elif i == 2:
                t = min(t, 0.15 * rem)
            else:
                t = min(t, 0.10 * rem)

            t = max(3.0, t)
            p_new, s_new = optimize_candidate(a, rng, max_time=t, end_time=hard_deadline)
            if np.isfinite(s_new):
                p_new = normalize_sequence(p_new)
                # Re-score after basis/refinement to guard against numerical drift.
                s_check = fast_score(p_new)
                if np.isfinite(s_check) and s_check <= s_new + 1e-9:
                    s_new = s_check
                update_best_candidate(p_new, s_new)

    # Final polish on the current best, if time remains.
    if global_best is not None:
        rem = max(0.0, hard_deadline - 10.0 - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 1.0:
            p_pol, s_pol = polish_local(global_best, rng, max_time=rem)
            if np.isfinite(s_pol):
                p_pol = normalize_sequence(p_pol)
                s_check = fast_score(p_pol)
                if np.isfinite(s_check) and s_check <= s_pol + 1e-9:
                    s_pol = s_check
                update_best_candidate(p_pol, s_pol)

    if global_best is None:
        global_best = make_power_profile(2048, 0.5, 0.0)
        global_best_score = fast_score(global_best)

    # Exact final selection among the best fast-scored candidates.
    final_candidates = [global_best]
    for s, a in selected[:6]:
        final_candidates.append(a)

    final_best = None
    final_best_exact = float("inf")
    exact_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, min(60.0, 0.05 * budget_s))

    for a in final_candidates:
        a = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
        if a.size == 0:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() >= exact_deadline:
            ev = fast_score(a)
        else:
            ev = compute_exact_score_sequence(a)
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < final_best_exact:
            final_best_exact = float(ev)
            final_best = a.copy()

    if final_best is None:
        final_best = global_best

    final_best = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(final_best, dtype=np.float64))
    final_best = np.where(np.isfinite(final_best), final_best, 0.0)
    final_best = np.maximum(final_best, 0.0)
    final_best = np.minimum(final_best, 1000.0)

    # Final safety validation.
    try:
        val = compute_exact_score_sequence(final_best)
        print(f"final_score={val:.10f} length={final_best.size}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(final_best)):
        final_best = make_power_profile(2048, 0.5, 0.0)

    return [float(x) for x in final_best]
```
